# Refero Styles — DESIGN.md Catalog

A comprehensive local library of **1342** production design systems and `DESIGN.md` files scraped from [styles.refero.design](https://styles.refero.design/).

Each file contains design tokens (colors, typography, spacing, radius, shadows), component rules, and implementation guidance ready for use with AI coding assistants (Claude Code, Cursor, Codex, Gemini, v0, Lovable).

## Quick Stats

- **Total Styles:** 1342
- **Themes:** Dark: 293, Light: 916, Mixed: 133
